documents and dated run reports supply detail; they do not independently set an
axis's current status.

## How to interpret and update this record

Implementation, workflow validation, shared-root integration and later extensions
are different scopes. A valid unavailable scientific value is an output, not by
itself an unfinished implementation. Source review establishes the connections
described below; it does not establish successful execution of changed code.
Older passing runs apply to their recorded source and scope.

Before changing a status, inspect the relevant executable code and, for an
execution claim, the actual retained result or receipt. Record the scope, date,
basis and remaining work in this ledger. Preserve previous milestones in the
change history. A later extension or an older unsuccessful case does not reopen
a completed implementation. A regression in that implementation needs a specific
code/result finding. Do not infer completeness from module names, test counts,
job IDs, process exit codes or availability notices.

The code-review manifest, [EVIDENCE_AXIS_CODE_REVIEW.sha256](EVIDENCE_AXIS_CODE_REVIEW.sha256),
identifies the reviewed source bytes. A changed file means the affected review
needs refreshing; it does not automatically invalidate every earlier milestone.
When updating that manifest, retain its old relevant identity in the dated change
entry. No tests, Python/import/syntax checks, replays, investigations, native jobs
or remote commands were executed for this appraisal. A102/A103 integration tests
remain deferred under the standing instruction.

## Shared action/result/credit reporting — source implementation, verification deferred

On 2026-11-07 the user requested the first communication-audit issue fixed for
A101–A104, A107/A110 and the other shared report consumers, with a separate
subagent for each axis. All six axis presentation modules and the shared
`episode_work_report.py` path are written. Original selected work, actual
returned results or concrete failures, and original receiver/parent completion
credit appear together. Saved scientific quantities and explicitly delivered
child-local credit retain their own meanings. Received names resolve in place;
the report creates no new judgment, scientific score, credit event or action.

The shared runtime creates this view after the receiver's authorized native
views are assembled. Scientific/root, selected execution, RNA, monitoring,
API, HPC and generic/component literature presentations receive the linked
report. Existing A105/A108 paired-work views are refreshed through their actual
decision hooks and retained without duplicate reports. Native immutable grants
match their original publication; experimental current views additionally
require the originally delivered sheet/component selection, preserving partial
returns and preventing later deliveries from entering an older action's result.

This is source implementation and manual code inspection only. The user
explicitly deferred all verification until the remaining communication-audit
bullets are fixed. No imports, tests, dry runs, retained-run reconstruction,
workflow, native algorithm or HPC execution was performed for this change.
Earlier axis milestones and retained execution results remain unchanged. The
other audit bullets remain separate work; this entry does not mark them fixed.

### Additional communication source changes, 2026-11-07

A106 now presents its actual saved experiment, conditions and numerical cells
together. Cell-label dictionaries and separate numerical-definition lookups
are removed from its active prompt renderer. Original values, units, selected
versions, limitations and main-sheet contributor cells remain explicit.

New default A101–A104, merged A102 and A107/A110 returns use the saved grading
and host-bound numerical facts instead of requiring an additional agent-selected
support verdict. Existing immutable legacy forms remain readable; their extra
verdicts are not repeated in current resolved grading reports. No new numerical
threshold or scientific classification is introduced.

API failure feedback now includes the complete exception message, returned
diagnostics and received error-response body. API prompts and paired action
reports preserve those errors rather than applying operational field filters,
body compaction or publication-failure previews. Native provider failure states
retain the actual returned error as well. Original error artifacts and original
action credit remain separate facts; neither is replaced or recalculated.

Shared-information offers no longer accept an agent-written evidence-summary
paragraph. Fulfilled and partially fulfilled replies select evidence without a
separate assessment; unavailable/superseded replies retain their operational
reason, and requests retain the missing operand and decision affected. Before
receipt, only offered-material metadata are shown. After the ordinary shared
read, the host copies exact selected saved grading/scoring results from the
receiver's existing authorized views, retaining values, units, conditions and
limitations. Existing native grading detail readers supply omitted detail
pages; no new grading criterion, score or credit event is introduced. The
snapshot belongs to the original action and appears beside its recorded earned
credit. Later deliveries cannot enter that historical payload. Material without
delivered grading is identified as material only. Old authored paragraphs remain
in immutable artifacts but are omitted from current message presentation.

These changes are source implementation only. All execution and verification
remain deferred at the user's instruction. The HPC instruction-led execution
replacement and its native receipt integrations are still being reconciled;
the shared-information issue now has source implementation, with validation
still deferred. No previous
axis validation milestone is reopened or upgraded by these presentation edits.

## A102 — completed subtree and shared-root milestone

The subtree is complete in implementation. Its earlier composition with A101
under a shared parent is also completed work. Do not replace either milestone
with an axis-wide incomplete status because later changes await execution.

| Milestone | Implementation / delivery status | Validation scope |
|---|---|---|
| Original comparative A102 subtree | Complete | Real structural comparisons, automatic grading and matching owner/parent credit are retained. |
| Higher-level A101/A102 composition | Complete for the delivered shared-root wiring | Real phmmer/US-align jobs, shared-parent information exchange, original numerical custody and offset-product root credit are retained. Episode choices in these wiring cases were scripted. |
| Experimental structural evidence merged into A102 | Implemented, including independent experimental tasks and structured parent reports | The newly merged full-workflow validation remains deferred. |
| Current shared-HPC connection | Implemented, including authenticated result admission and automatic attribution | The newly connected full-workflow validation remains deferred. |

The shared-root implementation is
[evidence_root.py](../gene_annotation_episode/evidence_root.py): `_projector`
binds the independent A101 and A102 projectors, and
`NativeEvidenceRootCredit.read_details` recomputes their contributions from the
receiving parent's actual receipts. With those two axes, the root applies
`A101 + A102 + A101*A102`. The
[two-wing fixture](../tests/evidence_root_fixture.py) constructs A101/A102
siblings, passes an A102 information need through the root to A101, returns the
sequence evidence and then commissions the structural follow-up. Its result
checks the structural return against the native B component and root projection.

The [saved root completion record](../runs/a101_a102_root_20261103_000215/FINAL_RESULTS.json)
identifies two completed real native wiring runs with automatic grading and
shared-parent need delivery. The saved case results preserve:

| Real case | A101 | A102 | Shared-root progress |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| [LacY](../runs/a101_a102_root_20261103_000215/lacy4/RESULTS.json) | 0.9131238899278988 | 0.6184373648168516 | 2.0962711869830732 |
| [AtpG](../runs/a101_a102_root_20261103_000215/atpg/RESULTS.json) | 0.9964119583531944 | 0.906466438520081 | 2.8060923960605146 |

Those original results predate later tool-specific attribution work, the
experimental-structure merge and current HPC additions. They establish the
completed original joint-root milestone within its recorded wiring scope;
they are not execution evidence for the later changes. The current source
contains the subsequent tool-/feature-specific attribution implementations.

[a102_structural_evidence.py](../gene_annotation_episode/a102_structural_evidence.py)
defines `a102_structural_evidence_v2`, independent comparative/experimental tasks
and task-specific progress. [a102_structural_workflow.py](../gene_annotation_episode/a102_structural_workflow.py)
exposes `A102StructuralEvidenceServiceFactory`, `A102StructuralEvidenceSubtree`
and `create_a102_structural_evidence_workflow`. The configured factory enables
the existing experimental literature operations and composes the current
execution service; it does not install a second HPC owner.

[a102_tools.py](../gene_annotation_episode/a102_tools.py),
[a102_hpc.py](../gene_annotation_episode/a102_hpc.py) and
[a102_structural_bridge.py](../gene_annotation_episode/a102_structural_bridge.py)
connect exact requests and authenticated results to the original comparison
publisher/grader. [a102_attachments.py](../gene_annotation_episode/a102_attachments.py)
retains tool-, region-, feature- and interface-specific attribution.
The runtime calls admission/attribution before projection.

The independent experimental route remains `literature.structure.populate` →
[literature_structure_storage.py](../gene_annotation_episode/literature_structure_storage.py)
→ exact `literature_structure_cells` delivery.
[a102_experimental_evidence.py](../gene_annotation_episode/a102_experimental_evidence.py)
validates the delivered subset and retains original sheet/component identity,
experiment/model identity, validation and provenance.
[a102_structural_report.py](../gene_annotation_episode/a102_structural_report.py)
`resolve_report` and `received_reports` use the actual receiver's receipts;
the scientific parent's fresh-call hook presents those resolved reports.
Experimental results require no alignment or B score. The six existing geometry
components and residue-RSCC limitation remain unchanged.

The earlier [comparative model results](../runs/a102_20261102_215317/MODEL_RESULTS.json)
show automatic grading before credit inspection and equal owner/parent credit
for TPI, LacY, AtpG and MJ0226. Those runs predate the merged/HPC additions.
The current merge/HPC scope is implemented, with full comparative-only,
experimental-only, shared-structure, missing/conflicting and legacy cases still
deferred. `combined_scientific_score` is deliberately null: B and experimental
geometry have no agreed common scalar. This is an explicit scientific boundary,
not authorization to substitute B or controller progress.

scoring sheets synchronously before the original action returns. The existing
scientific calculators and declared metric bindings are retained; absent metric
bindings remain unresolved. A retained replay of all77 actual A10513 grading
publications saves77 automatic main sheets with no regression of previously

original A10513 trace is unchanged. Results are under
`runs/automatic_literature_sheet_publication_replay_20261107_01/A105/`.

Possessed scientific names and same-action aliases are resolved by the host;

This status is supported by the executable path, not merely the earlier user
completion statement. [literature_assay_grading_inputs.py](../gene_annotation_episode/literature_assay_grading_inputs.py)
`read_assay_grading_inputs` accepts actual source/readers and authenticated
database measurements. [literature_assay_grading.py](../gene_annotation_episode/literature_assay_grading.py)
declares eight supported assay types and implements `grade_assay` /
`grade_experimental_measurement`, including the conditional signed log evidence
ratio and explicit missing-input reasons. The types cover activity rates,
endpoint conversion, product distribution, reaction coupling, ligand binding,
transport response, protein signals and protein amounts. Their scientific
quantity meanings and conditions remain distinct.

[literature_assay_grading_storage.py](../gene_annotation_episode/literature_assay_grading_storage.py)
`LiteratureAssayGradingRepository` binds the actual readers, calculator, saved
sheet, experiment identity, typed cells and receipt validation.
[literature_a105_aggregation.py](../gene_annotation_episode/literature_a105_aggregation.py)
`aggregate_a105` implements the agreed reducers: reliability SUM, validity SUM,
relevance MAX, attribution MAX, overall SUM of the selected experiment overall
values. It verifies experiment/assertion/component correspondence, rejects
duplicate experiments and preserves partial/unavailable results.
[literature_a105_aggregation_storage.py](../gene_annotation_episode/literature_a105_aggregation_storage.py)
authenticates possessed source components, saves the exact aggregation, and
exposes only the owned or delivered subset.

[literature_episodes.py](../gene_annotation_episode/literature_episodes.py)
connects `literature.assay_grading.populate` and `literature.a105.aggregate` to
those repositories; advertises `literature_assay_grading_cells` and
`literature_a105_aggregation_cells`; validates requested numerical returns;
records exact cells through `received_form_references`; and exposes admitted
cells to the receiving parent through `received_report_facts`.
[literature_rate_storage.py](../gene_annotation_episode/literature_rate_storage.py)
enforces owned versus actually received numerical custody.
`bind_assembled_services` and saved assembled case policy preserve explicit
`literature_configuration`; the supported A105 operations require the existing
`rate_population` and `assay_grading_population` configuration. No missing
scoring/aggregation/typed-parent-delivery implementation stage was found.

The [database integration evidence](../runs/a105_database_integration_2026-11-02_014/completion-evidence.json)
is a dated extension record. It includes real source recovery and aggregation
attempts, with unresolved inputs; earlier full-scoring attempts were stopped or
failed. These facts must remain visible as validation limitations for those
cases. They do not establish a regression in the completed implementation and
do not authorize relabeling A105 as unfinished or declaring a database extension
currently active. Fresh full-workflow completion for every newer extension is
not claimed by this source appraisal.

## A106 — implemented path, broader completion still open

[literature_intervention_inputs.py](../gene_annotation_episode/literature_intervention_inputs.py)
and the perturbation readers consume source measurements or authenticated
native intervention-analysis results.
[literature_intervention_grading.py](../gene_annotation_episode/literature_intervention_grading.py)
reuses the existing signed experimental comparison machinery.
[literature_a106_subscore.py](../gene_annotation_episode/literature_a106_subscore.py)
`finalize_a106` binds the subject, assertion, alternative and biological scope;
retains current experiment assessments, signed values and original measurement
keys; and conditions cross-experiment summation on supported independence.
[literature_a106_subscore_storage.py](../gene_annotation_episode/literature_a106_subscore_storage.py)
retains question/revision custody. `LiteratureEpisodes` exposes grading,
`literature.a106.finalize`, typed result cells and the same parent receipt path.
The assembled service and saved policy preserve the intervention configuration.

Inspection of a possible alternate HPC analysis backend confirmed that current
A106 child access and its live evaluation factory bind analysis to AppService.
The native intervention module reads result tables; the shared HPC commissioning
helper supplies scheduling inputs rather than a TRANSIT/DESeq2 worker. The
inspected A107 expression worker calculates declared table components, not
differential expression. Switching a resource setting alone therefore cannot
demonstrate A106 dataset analysis. The alternate backend would require an actual
worker and authenticated A106 request/return bindings; it remains unimplemented.
This source inspection does not reopen the existing BV-BRC adapter implementation
or claim a remote job, live service result or numerical score.

Four checks additionally exercise the existing same-row requirement with valid
but distinct DESeq2 measurements, including repeated gene names, through both
retained-file and received-application paths. They add no new scoring rule.

## A107 — existing experimental path, incomplete broader axis

2026-11-07 source correction and custody checks: the requirement adapter previously
checked subject/partner and shared paper without checking the selected sheet's
experiment identity. It now requires an explicit observation-to-experiment
link, permits explicitly linked source representations and preserves numerical
components with unresolved application when identity/linkage is missing.
Historical graded bodies and numbers remain readable; old component-bearing
qualifications lacking this check are marked for update rather than treated as
verified experiment attribution. The operation description exposes that
scientific requirement. The cases in
`tests/test_a107_requirement_experiment_binding.py` are now exercised by the
combined experiment-binding custody checks02/03 described in the active-goal
section. Those checks use original numerical population, typed cell admission
and qualification grading; they are not autonomous primary-paper validation.
Interrupted A11019 retained its original frozen bytes. The corrected adapters
are included in the subsequent frozen implementations used by A107 region02/03;
region03 verifies the independent expression/region path, not this particular
experimental-requirement association. The A107 primary-paper workflow remains
prepared and unstarted. This advances source implementation and scoped custody
validation without claiming axis completion.

First implementation step, 2026-11-05: source entry points now exist in
[a107_investigation.py](../gene_annotation_episode/a107_investigation.py).
[component_axis_workflow.py](../gene_annotation_episode/component_axis_workflow.py)
adds the independent service/subtree/task construction, composes the existing
execution service and keeps other scientific axes under the shared parent.
[component_axis_reports.py](../gene_annotation_episode/component_axis_reports.py)
adds exact delivered-subset interpretation, numerical identity/dependence and
typed parent report resolution. Narrow scientific binding hooks supply task
guidance, same-axis descendants, finding progress and parent report handling.
These initial additions had source review only; no imports, syntax checks,
workflows or numerical execution ran.

Second source step, 2026-11-05: [a107_tools.py](../gene_annotation_episode/a107_tools.py)
and [a107_qualification.py](../gene_annotation_episode/a107_qualification.py) add
particular source-requirement, received sequence/region, scoped detection and
measured-expression uses. Genomic work reuses the original A103 HPC adapter as
tools; A103 remains a scientific sibling. Direct local native genomic work is
disabled in the new registry. [a107_expression_components.py](../gene_annotation_episode/a107_expression_components.py)
adds selected matched-condition pairs and descriptive Pearson components over
the declared source-table format. A measured RNA feature needs its actual

The signed profile preserves missing values as `None`, measured neutral values
as zero, and actual opposing evidence as negative. Missing homology information
does not refute a functional assertion. Complete A105/A106 log evidence uses
the existing tanh-half-log-ratio convention; the new A107 softsign/bottleneck
and A108 observation-count softening are explicit root conventions, not changes
to native graders. Applicability and original scope are required in either
direction. Supporting values and opposing magnitudes are multiplied separately
per target before the length-weighted means; the score is support minus opposition.
The configured bounds are [-31, 511], since only five of the nine axes can oppose.

The split-combination change passed **78** focused checks on 2026-11-09 in
`runs/split_signed_contributions_checks_20261109_01/` (JUnit `results.xml`,
25.42 seconds; `workflow-result.json` return code 0 and descendant drain confirmed).
Those checks cover arithmetic, attribution, actual receipt handling, and ordinary
context/summary propagation with synthetic inputs. No launcher, episode
orchestration, native mapping, continuation policy or genome run changed.

Earlier verification of the original combination on 2026-11-09: all **70** focused scalar, receiving, revision,
shared-runtime and case-policy checks pass in
`runs/all_axes_root_final_checks_20261109_02/` (JUnit `results.xml`, completed
`workflow-result.json`, return code 0 and descendant drain confirmed). They use
explicitly synthetic scientific inputs with actual native calculators and
receiving APIs; they do not establish biological correctness of all mappings.
Nine failures in the broader existing root-boundary/receipt suites reproduce
with only this task's runtime edits removed in an isolated source copy; the
failure sets match exactly in
`runs/all_axes_root_final_checks_20261109_01/prechange-comparison.json`.
These existing failures and deferred per-axis validation remain separate.
No frozen workflow was restarted or reconfigured for this integration.
